newPod.Status.Phase)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t},\n\t\tDeleteFunc: func(obj interface{}) {\n\t\t\tpod := obj.(*corev1.Pod)\n\t\t\tlog.Info(\"Pod deleted\", \"name\", some environments where the network isn't reliable.\n\t// Let's retry when that happens. adjustResources(ctx, actualStatefulSets, expectedResources)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\t// reconcile all resources\n\tfor _, res := range adjusted {\n\t\tif err := settings.ReconcileConfig(ctx.k8sClient, ctx.es, res.StatefulSet.Name, res.Config); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, err\n\t\t}\n\t\tif _, err := common.ReconcileService(ctx.parentCtx, ctx.k8sClient, &res.HeadlessService, &ctx.es); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, err\n\t\t}\n\t\treconciled, err := sset.ReconcileStatefulSet(ctx.k8sClient, ctx.es, res.StatefulSet, ctx.expectations)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, err\n\t\t}\n\t\t// update actual with the reconciled ones for next steps to work with up-to-date information\n\t\tactualStatefulSets = actualStatefulSets.WithStatefulSet(reconciled)\n\t}\n\treturn actualStatefulSets, nil\n}\n\nfunc adjustResources(\n\tctx upscaleCtx,\n\tactualStatefulSets sset.StatefulSetList,\n\texpectedResources nodespec.ResourcesList,\n) (nodespec.ResourcesList, error) {\n\tupscaleState := newUpscaleState(ctx, actualStatefulSets, expectedResources)\n\tadjustedResources := make(nodespec.ResourcesList, 0, len(expectedResources))\n\tfor _, nodeSpecRes := range expectedResources {\n\t\tadjusted, err := adjustStatefulSetReplicas(upscaleState, actualStatefulSets, *nodeSpecRes.StatefulSet.DeepCopy())\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, err\n\t\t}\n\t\tnodeSpecRes.StatefulSet = adjusted\n\t\tadjustedResources = append(adjustedResources, nodeSpecRes)\n\t}\n\t// adapt resources configuration to match adjusted replicas\n\tif err := adjustZenConfig(ctx.k8sClient, ctx.es, adjustedResources); err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\treturn adjustedResources, nil\n}\n\nfunc adjustZenConfig(k8sClient k8s.Client, es esv1.Elasticsearch, resources nodespec.ResourcesList) error {\n\t// patch configs to consider zen1 minimum master nodes\n\tif err := zen1.SetupMinimumMasterNodesConfig(k8sClient, es, resources); err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\t// patch configs to consider zen2 initial master nodes\n\tif err := zen2.SetupInitialMasterNodes(es, k8sClient, resources); err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// adjustStatefulSetReplicas updates the replicas count in expected according to\n// what is allowed by the upscaleState, that may be mutated as a result.\nfunc adjustStatefulSetReplicas(\n\tupscaleState *upscaleState,\n\tactualStatefulSets sset.StatefulSetList,\n\texpected appsv1.StatefulSet,\n) (appsv1.StatefulSet, error) {\n\tactual, alreadyExists := actualStatefulSets.GetByName(expected.Name)\n\texpectedReplicas := sset.GetReplicas(expected)\n\tactualReplicas := sset.GetReplicas(actual)\n\n\tif actualReplicas < expectedReplicas {\n\t\treturn upscaleState.limitNodesCreation(actual, expected)\n\t}\n\n\tif alreadyExists && expectedReplicas < actualReplicas {\n\t\t// this is a downscale.\n\t\t// We still want to update the sset spec to the newest one, but leave scaling down as it's done later.\n\t\tnodespec.UpdateReplicas(&expected, actual.Spec.Replicas)\n\t}\n\n\treturn expected, nil\n}\n\n// isReplicaIncrease returns true if expected replicas are higher than actual replicas.\nfunc isReplicaIncrease(actual appsv1.StatefulSet, expected appsv1.StatefulSet) bool {\n\treturn sset.GetReplicas(expected) > sset.GetReplicas(actual)\n}\n","numLines":131,"startLine":1,"totalLines":131}}}

eg. v6 to v7 do not need that setting.\n// It ensures `cluster.initial_master_nodes` does not vary over time, when this function gets called multiple times.\nfunc SetupInitialMasterNodes(es esv1.Elasticsearch, k8sClient k8s.Client, nodeSpecResources nodespec.ResourcesList) error {\n\t// if the cluster is annotated with `cluster.initial_master_nodes` (zen2 bootstrap in progress),\n\t// len(initialMasterNodes) == 0 {\n\t\treturn pkgerrors.Errorf(\"no master node found to compute `cluster.initial_master_nodes`\")\n\t}\n\tlog.Info(\n\t\t\"Setting patchInitialMasterNodesConfig(nodeSpecResources, initialMasterNodes); err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\t// keep the computed value in an annotation for reuse in subsequent reconciliations\n\treturn error) {\n\tif v, err := version.Parse(es.Spec.Version); err != nil || !versionCompatibleWithZen2(*v) {\n\t\t// we only care about zen2-compatible clusters here\n\t\treturn false, err\n\t}\n\t// we want to set `cluster.initial_master_nodes` if:\n\t// - a new cluster is getting created (not already bootstrapped)\n\tif !bootstrap.AnnotatedForBootstrap(es) {\n\t\treturn true, nil\n\t}\n\t// - we're upgrading (effectively restarting) a single zen1 master to zen2\n\treturn singleZen1MasterUpgrade(k8sClient, es, nodeSpecResources)\n}\n\n// RemoveZen2BootstrapAnnotation removes the initialMasterNodesAnnotation (if set) once zen2 is bootstrapped\n// on the corresponding cluster.\nfunc RemoveZen2BootstrapAnnotation(ctx context.Context, k8sClient k8s.Client, es esv1.Elasticsearch, esClient client.Client) (bool, error) {\n\tif v, err := version.Parse(es.Spec.Version); err != nil || !versionCompatibleWithZen2(*v) {\n\t\t// we only care about zen2-compatible clusters here\n\t\treturn false, err\n\t}\n\tif getInitialMasterNodesAnnotation(es) == nil {\n\t\t// most common case: no annotation set, nothing to do\n\t\treturn false, nil\n\t}\n\t// the cluster was annotated to indicate it is performing a zen2 bootstrap,\n\t// let's check if that bootstrap is done so we can remove the annotation\n\tctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, client.DefaultReqTimeout)\n\tdefer cancel()\n\tisBootstrapped, err := esClient.ClusterBootstrappedForZen2(ctx)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn false, err\n\t}\n\tif !isBootstrapped {\n\t\t// retry later\n\t\treturn true, nil\n\t}\n\tlog.Info(\"Zen 2 bootstrap is complete\",\n\t\t\"namespace\", es.Namespace,\n\t\t\"es_name\", es.Name,\n\t)\n\t// remove the annotation to indicate we're done with zen2 bootstrapping\n\tdelete(es.Annotations, initialMasterNodesAnnotation)\n\treturn false, k8sClient.Update(&es)\n}\n\n// patchInitialMasterNodesConfig mutates the configuration of zen2-compatible master nodes\n// to have the given `cluster.initial_master_nodes` setting.\nfunc patchInitialMasterNodesConfig(nodeSpecResources nodespec.ResourcesList, initialMasterNodes []string) error {\n\tfor i, res := range nodeSpecResources {\n\t\tif !label.IsMasterNodeSet(res.StatefulSet) || !IsCompatibleWithZen2(res.StatefulSet) {\n\t\t\t// we only care about updating zen2 masters config here\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tif err := nodeSpecResources[i].Config.SetStrings(esv1.ClusterInitialMasterNodes, initialMasterNodes...); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// singleZen1MasterUpgrade returns true if expected nodes in nodeSpecResources will lead to upgrading\n// the single zen1-compatible master node currently running in the es cluster.\nfunc
